Reliability Diagram（校准图）\n93\t\n94\t**用途**：显示模型 confidence bin vs accuracy，对角线 = 完美校准。\n95\t\n96\t```python\n97\tdef plot_reliability_diagram(confidences, accuracies, n_bins=10,\n98\t                              label='', color='#0072B2',\n99\t                              title='Reliability Diagram',\n100\t                              outpath='reliability.pdf'):\n101\t    \"\"\"\n102\t    confidences, accuracies: 各样本的 confidence 和 binary correct (0/1)\n103\t    \"\"\"\n104\t    bin_edges = np.linspace(0, 1, n_bins + 1)\n105\t    bin_accs, bin_confs, bin_counts = [], [], []\n106\t\n107\t    for i in range(n_bins):\n108\t        lo, hi = bin_edges[i], bin_edges[i+1]\n109\t        mask = (confidences >= lo) & (confidences < hi)\n110\t        if mask.sum() > 0:\n111\t            bin_accs.append(accuracies[mask].mean())\n112\t            bin_confs.append(confidences[mask].mean())\n113\t            bin_counts.append(mask.sum())\n114\t        else:\n115\t            bin_accs.append(float('nan'))\n116\t            bin_confs.append((lo + hi) / 2)\n117\t            bin_counts.append(0)\n118\t\n119\t    bin_accs = np.array(bin_accs)\n120\t    bin_confs = np.array(bin_confs)\n121\t\n122\t    fig, (ax_main, ax_hist) = plt.subplots(\n123\t        2, 1, figsize=(4, 4.5), gridspec_kw={'height_ratios': [3, 1]},\n124\t        sharex=True\n125\t    )\n126\t\n127\t    # 主图：gaps\n128\t    ax_main.bar(bin_edges[:-1], bin_accs, width=1/n_bins, align='edge',\n129\t                color=color, alpha=0.7, label='Accuracy', zorder=2)\n130\t    ax_main.bar(bin_edges[:-1], bin_confs - bin_accs, width=1/n_bins, align='edge',\n131\t                bottom=bin_accs, color='red', alpha=0.3, label='Gap', zorder=2)\n132\t    ax_main.plot([0, 1], [0, 1], 'k--', linewidth=1, zorder=3, label='Perfect')\n133\t    ax_main.set_xlim(0, 1); ax_main.set_ylim(0, 1)\n134\t    ax_main.set_ylabel('Accuracy')\n135\t    ax_main.set_title(title)\n136\t    ax_main.legend(loc='upper left', fontsize=9)\n137\t    ax_main.grid(True, alpha=0.3, zorder=1)\n138\t\n139\t    # ECE annotation\n140\t    ece = np.nansum(np.array(bin_counts) / sum(bin_counts) *\n141\t                    np.abs(bin_accs - bin_confs))\n142\t    ax_main.text(0.98, 0.02, f'ECE={ece:.3f}', transform=ax_main.transAxes,\n143\t                 ha='right', va='bottom', fontsize=9,\n144\t                 bbox=dict(boxstyle='round,pad=0.3', facecolor='white', alpha=0.8))\n145\t\n146\t    # 直方图（样本密度）\n147\t    ax_hist.bar(bin_edges[:-1], bin_counts, width=1/n_bins, align='edge',\n148\t                color=color, alpha=0.5)\n149\t    ax_hist.set_ylabel('Count', fontsize=9)\n150\t    ax_hist.set_xlabel('Confidence')\n151\t\n152\t    plt.tight_layout()\n153\t    plt.savefig(outpath)\n154\t    plt.close()\n155\t    print(f'Saved: {outpath}')\n156\t```\n157\t\n158\t### 批量生成 6 张对比图（EMNLP 论文用）\n159\t\n160\t选图策略：每 Regime 选最大 ΔECE 的量化方法 vs FP16，LLaMA + Qwen3 各一。\n161\t\n162\t```python\n163\t# 在 A6000 服务器上执行\n164\tFIGURE_PLAN = [\n165\t    # (dataset, model, quant, regime, title)\n166\t    ('hellaswag',   'llama',  'nf4',  'A', 'HellaSwag (Regime A) — LLaMA'),\n167\t    ('hellaswag',   'qwen3',  'nf4',  'A', 'HellaSwag (Regime A) — Qwen3'),\n168\t    ('arc_c',       'llama',  'gptq', 'B', 'ARC-C (Regime B) — LLaMA'),\n169\t    ('arc_c',       'qwen3',  'gptq', 'B', 'ARC-C (Regime B) — Qwen3'),\n170\t    ('truthfulqa',  'llama',  'awq',  'C', 'TruthfulQA (Regime C) — LLaMA'),\n171\t    ('truthfulqa',  'qwen3',  'awq',  'C', 'TruthfulQA (Regime C) — Qwen3'),\n172\t]\n173\t```\n174\t\n175\t---\n176\t\n177\t## 2. ECE Heatmap（方法 × 数据集 ΔECE）\n178\t\n179\t**用途**：Main Results Table 的可视化版本，颜色 = ΔECE（红=恶化，蓝=改善）。\n180\t\n181\t```python\n182\tdef plot_ece_heatmap(delta_ece_matrix, methods, datasets,\n183\t                     title='ΔECE Heatmap (vs FP16)',\n184\t                     outpath='ece_heatmap.pdf'):\n185\t    \"\"\"\n186\t    delta_ece_matrix: shape (n_methods, n_datasets), 正=ECE 恶化, 负=改善\n187\t    \"\"\"\n188\t    import matplotlib.colors as mcolors\n189\t\n190\t    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(len(datasets) * 1.2, len(methods) * 0.8 + 1))\n191\t\n192\t    # 发散色图（中心=0, 红=恶化, 蓝=改善）\n193\t    vmax = np.nanmax(np.abs(delta_ece_matrix))\n194\t    norm = mcolors.TwoSlopeNorm(vmin=-vmax, vcenter=0, vmax=vmax)\n195\t    cmap = plt.cm.RdBu_r\n196\t\n197\t    im = ax.imshow(delta_ece_matrix, cmap=cmap, norm=norm, aspect='auto')\n198\t\n199\t    # 数值标注\n200\t    for i in range(len(methods)):\n201\t        for j in range(len(datasets)):\n202\t            val = delta_ece_matrix[i, j]\n203\t            if not np.isnan(val):\n204\t                color = 'white' if abs(val) > vmax * 0.6 else 'black'\n205\t                sign = '+' if val > 0 else ''\n206\t                ax.text(j, i, f'{sign}{val:.1%}', ha='center', va='center',\n207\t                        fontsize=9, color=color, fontweight='bold')\n208\t\n209\t    ax.set_xticks(range(len(datasets)))\n210\t    ax.set_yticks(range(len(methods)))\n211\t    ax.set_xticklabels(datasets, rotation=30, ha='right')\n212\t    ax.set_yticklabels(methods)\n213\t    ax.set_title(title)\n214\t\n215\t    cbar = plt.colorbar(im, ax=ax, fraction=0.046, pad=0.04)\n216\t    cbar.set_label('ΔECE (quantized − FP16)')\n217\t    cbar.ax.axhline(0, color='k', linewidth=0.8)\n218\t\n219\t    plt.tight_layout()\n220\t    plt.savefig(outpath)\n221\t    plt.close()\n222\t    print(f'Saved: {outpath}')\n223\t```\n224\t\n225\t---\n226\t\n227\t## 3. T* Scatter Plot（任务 Regime 可视化）\n228\t\n229\t**用途**：展示 5 个模型的 FP16 T* 分布，证明 Regime 跨模型一致性。\n230\t\n231\t```python\n232\tdef plot_tstar_scatter(tstar_data, outpath='tstar_scatter.pdf'):\n233\t zorder=1)\n251\t\n252\t    model_colors = [COLORS.get(m.split('-')[0].lower(), '#333333') for m in MODELS]\n253\t\n254\t    x = np.arange(len(DATASETS))\n255\t    for mi, (model, color) in va='center',\n329\t            fontsize=9, color='gray', zorder=1)\n330\t\n331\t# 单一 collapse zone\n332\tax.axhspan(-5, 5, color='#FDEDEC', alpha=0.5, zorder=0)\n333\tax.text(0.02, 2.5, 'collapse zone', fontsize=8,\n334\t        color='#ccaaaa', fontstyle='italic', va='center')\n335\t```\n336\t\n337\t### Pattern B：发散面积 fill_between\n338\t\n339\t```python\n340\t# alpha 极低（0.06）：不抢眼但有面积\n341\tax.fill_between(x_range, curve_upper, curve_lower,\n342\t                alpha=0.06, color='#555555', zorder=1, label='_nolegend_')\n343\t```\n344\t\n345\t### Pattern C：参考线锚定真实值\n346\t\n347\t```python\n348\tax.axhline(y=actual_value, color=color, linestyle=':',\n349\t           linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.3, zorder=1)\n350\tt = ax.text(x_pos, actual_value + 1, 'actual W3A16',\n351\t            fontsize=7, color=color, alpha=0.5, ha='center')\n352\tt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\n353\t```\n354\t\n355\t### Pattern D：信息层级 marker（hollow vs solid by sample size）\n356\t\n357\t```python\n358\t# 实心 = n >= 50, 空心带 n 标注 = n < 50\n359\tsolid_mask = [n >= 50 for n in n_vals]\n360\tfor x, y, n, solid in zip(xs, ys, n_vals, solid_mask):\n361\t    if solid:\n362\t        ax.plot(x, y, marker=marker, color=color, markersize=6,\n363\t                markerfacecolor=color, markeredgewidth=0)\n364\t    else:\n365\t        ax.plot(x, y, marker=marker, color=color, markersize=6,\n366\t                markerfacecolor='white', markeredgecolor=color,\n367\t                markeredgewidth=1.2, zorder=4)\n368\t        ax.annotate(f'n={n}', (x, y), textcoords='offset points',\n369\t                    xytext=(0, 7), fontsize=5, color=color,\n370\t                    ha='center', fontstyle='italic')\n371\t```\n372\t\n373\t### Pattern E：Wilson Binomial CI\n374\t\n375\t```python\n376\tdef wilson_ci(p, n, z=1.96):\n377\t    if n == 0: return 0, 0\n378\t    denom = 1 + z**2 / n\n379\t    center = (p + z**2 / (2*n)) / denom\n380\t    half = z * np.sqrt((p*(1-p) + z**2/(4*n)) / n) / denom\n381\t    return max(0, center - half), min(1, center + half)\n382\t\n383\t# 用法\n384\tlo, hi = wilson_ci(accuracy, n)\n385\tax.errorbar(x, accuracy*100, yerr=[[accuracy*100 - lo*100], [hi*100 - accuracy*100]],\n386\t            fmt='o', capsize=4, capthick=0.8, elinewidth=0.8,\n387\t            markeredgecolor='white', markeredgewidth=1.0)\n388\t```\n389\t\n390\t### Pattern F：Per-label offset dict（防标注重叠）\n391\t\n392\t```python\n393\tlabel_offsets = {\n394\t    'Qwen3-8B':  (6, -12),\n395\t    'Qwen3-4B':  (-55, 4),\n396\t    'Llama-8B':  (6, 6),\n397\t}\n398\tfor name, (x, y) in data.items():\n399\t    ox, oy = label_offsets.get(name, (6, 6))\n400\t    ax.annotate(name, (x, y), xytext=(ox, oy),\n401\t                textcoords='offset points', fontsize=7,\n402\t                arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='-', color='gray',\n403\t                                lw=0.4) if abs(ox) > 20 else None)\n404\t```\n405\t\n406\t### Pattern G：Hatching 加分类维度\n407\t\n408\t```python\n409\tfrom matplotlib.patches import Patch\n410\tfor is_collapse, bar in zip(collapse_flags, bars):\n411\t    if is_collapse:\n412\t        bar.set_hatch('///')\n413\t        bar.set_alpha(0.7)\n414\t# Legend 加 hatching 说明\n415\tlegend_patch = Patch(facecolor='gray', hatch='///', alpha=0.7, label='Collapse')\n416\tax.legend(handles=[legend_patch], loc='lower right')\n417\t```\n418\t\n419\t### Pattern H：Multi-panel 非对称布局\n420\t\n421\t```python\n422\tfig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(6.5, 3.2),\n423\t                          gridspec_kw={'width_ratios': [1.4, 1]})\n424\t# axes[0] 是主面板（1.4x 宽），axes[1] 副面板\n425\t# 给每个 panel 写 insight subtitle\n426\taxes[0].set_title('(a) Quantization shifts confidence', fontsize=9, pad=6)\n427\taxes[1].set_title('(b) Temperature scaling recovers it', fontsize=9, pad=6)\n428\t```\n429\t\n430\t### Pattern I：Broken axis（异常值或对数 0）\n431\t\n432\t```python\n433\td = 0.012  # 斜线长度\n434\tkwargs = dict(transform=ax.transAxes, color='black',\n435\t              clip_on=False, linewidth=0.6)\n436\tax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (-d, +d), **kwargs)\n437\tax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (1-d, 1+d), **kwargs)\n438\t```\n439\t\n440\t### Pattern J：Path-effect 文字描边\n441\t\n442\t```python\n443\timport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\n444\tt = ax.text(x, y, 'label', fontsize=8, color='red')\n445\tt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\n446\t```\n447\t\n448\t### Pattern K：Concept curve（合成轨迹建立直觉）\n449\t\n450\t```python\n451\t# 完全不基于实验数据的概念曲线\n452\tx = np.linspace(0, 1, 100)\n453\ttrajectory = baseline * (1 - np.cos(np.pi * x)) / 2\n454\tax.plot(x, trajectory, color='#0072B2', linewidth=2,\n455\t        solid_capstyle='round', label='Theoretical trajectory')\n456\t\n457\t# 关键节点用 insight 标注\n458\tax.annotate('gap eliminated', xy=(1.0, final_y),\n459\t            xytext=(0.7, final_y - 5), color='#388E3C',\n460\t            fontstyle='italic', fontsize=9,\n461\t            arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='->', color='#388E3C', lw=0.8))\n462\t```\n463\t\n464\t### Pattern L：Forest Plot（meta-analysis 风格）\n465\t\n466\t```python\n467\t# 3-column GridSpec: label | CI plot | inline stats\n468\tfig = plt.figure(figsize=(7, 3.5))\n469\tgs = fig.add_gridspec(1, 3, width_ratios=[0.8, 2, 1.2], wspace=0.1)\n470\tax_label = fig.add_subplot(gs[0]); ax_label.axis('off')\n471\tax_main = fig.add_subplot(gs[1])\n472\tax_stat = fig.add_subplot(gs[2]); ax_stat.axis('off')\n473\t\n474\t# 等价带（半透明 axvspan）\n475\tax_main.axvspan(-1, 1, alpha=0.1, color='gray', label='±1pp equivalence')\n476\t\n477\t# 每行：dot + horizontal CI line\n478\tfor i, (name, mean, lo, hi, pval) in enumerate(rows):\n479\t    ax_label.text(0.5, i, name, fontsize=9, va='center', ha='center')\n480\t    ax_main.plot([lo, hi], [i, i], color='black', linewidth=1)\n481\t    ax_main.plot(mean, i, 'D', color='#0072B2', markersize=7)\n482\t    ax_stat.text(0.5, i, f'{mean:+.2f} [{lo:+.2f}, {hi:+.2f}]  p={pval:.3f}',\n483\t                 fontsize=8, va='center', ha='center', family='monospace')\n484\t\n485\tax_main.axvline(0, color='black', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.6)\n486\tax_main.invert_yaxis()\n487\t```\n488\t\n489\t---\n490\t\n491\t## 6. 图表 Checklist（提交前）\n492\t\n493\t### L1 防丑（底线）\n494\t- [ ] 300 DPI（`savefig.dpi=300`）+ PDF 矢量\n495\t- [ ] 色盲友好配色（Okabe-Ito 或语义色板）\n496\t- [ ] 所有轴有 label + 单位\n497\t- [ ] 字号 ≥ 10pt（缩到 column width 后仍可读）\n498\t- [ ] 无 dual y-axis、无 3D、无 pie\n499\t- [ ] Bar chart 从 0 起（line/scatter 可非 0）\n500\t- [ ] 文字水平不旋转\n501\t\n502\t### L2 叙事（说服力）\n503\t- [ ] 能用一句话写出 figure takeaway（应等于 `narrative_intent`）\n504\t- [ ] `design_notes` 中的 pattern 都已实现\n505\t- [ ] Subtitle / panel label 是 insight 不是描述\n506\t- [ ] 数据质量差异视觉可见（n < 50 用空心 / OOS 用 diamond 等）\n507\t- [ ] matplotlib\nversion: 2\ntools:\n  - ssh_execute\n---\n\n# Draw Paper Figure — 学术统计图生成（Worker 执行）\n\nWorker 收到统计图画图任务时读本 skill。**画图前必读** `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 的 L1/L2 原则和 L3 pattern 库。\n\n**质量底线：每张图必须达到 NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP best paper 的视觉水准——高级感、专业感、精致感。绝不接受 matplotlib 默认样式的廉价感（灰背景、粗网格线、tab10 色板、默认蓝色）。**\n\n**生成图片后必须 Read PNG 做视觉检查，至少迭代 3 轮（Hero Figure 至少 5 轮）。第一版画出来基本不能直接用。**\n\n本 skill 提供：\n- 通用样式（rcParams + 色板）\n- 三个常用图表的起点模板（reliability/heatmap/scatter）\n- NeurIPS 经验提炼的 pattern 实现示例\n\n## 输入字段（从 Paper Agent 传入）\n\n- `fig_id` / `type` / `placement` / `data_source` / `aspect_ratio` / 输出路径\n- **`narrative_intent`**：一句话，这张图要让读者相信什么\n- **`design_notes`**：从 paper-visualization L3 选的 pattern 列表\n\n## 画图工作流\n\n1. 读 paper-visualization skill 的 L1 防丑原则 + L2 叙事原则\n2. 根据 `narrative_intent` 选起点模板（本 skill §模板）\n3. 按 `design_notes` 中每个 pattern 添加相应技法（本 skill §pattern 实现）\n4. 提交前过本 skill §checklist\n\n## 通用样式标准（所有图表必须遵守）\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport matplotlib.patches as mpatches\nimport numpy as np\n\n# 字体和全局样式\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 11,\n    'axes.titlesize': 13,\n    'axes.labelsize': 12,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 10,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 10,\n    'legend.fontsize': 10,\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,\n    'axes.spines.top': False,\n    'axes.spines.right': False,\n    'lines.linewidth': 1.8,\n})\n\n# 色盲友好配色：优先从 writing_plan.yaml 的 color_palette 读取\n# 读不到则 fallback 到以下默认值\nCOLORS = {\n    'fp16':   '#E69F00',  # 橙黄\n    'nf4':    '#56B4E9',  # 天蓝\n    'int8':   '#009E73',  # 绿\n    'gptq':   '#F0E442',  # 黄\n    'awq':    '#CC79A7',  # 粉紫\n    'llama':  '#0072B2',  # 深蓝\n    'qwen3':  '#D55E00',  # 橙红\n    'gemma':  '#009E73',  # 绿\n    'mistral':'#CC79A7',  # 粉紫\n    'phi3':   '#F0E442',  # 黄\n}\n\n# 方法标签\nMETHOD_LABELS = {\n    'fp16': 'FP16', 'nf4': 'BNB-NF4',\n    'int8': 'BNB-INT8', 'gptq': 'GPTQ-4bit', 'awq': 'AWQ-4bit'\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 1. Reliability Diagram（校准图）\n\n**用途**：显示模型 confidence bin vs accuracy，对角线 = 完美校准。\n\n```python\ndef plot_reliability_diagram(confidences, accuracies, n_bins=10,\n                              label='', color='#0072B2',\n                              title='Reliability Diagram',\n                              outpath='reliability.pdf'):\n    \"\"\"\n    confidences, accuracies: 各样本的 confidence 和 binary correct (0/1)\n    \"\"\"\n    bin_edges = np.linspace(0, 1, n_bins + 1)\n    bin_accs, bin_confs, bin_counts = [], [], []\n\n    for i in range(n_bins):\n        lo, hi = bin_edges[i], bin_edges[i+1]\n        mask = (confidences >= lo) & (confidences < hi)\n        if mask.sum() > 0:\n            bin_accs.append(accuracies[mask].mean())\n            bin_confs.append(confidences[mask].mean())\n            bin_counts.append(mask.sum())\n        else:\n            bin_accs.append(float('nan'))\n            bin_confs.append((lo + hi) / 2)\n            bin_counts.append(0)\n\n    bin_accs = np.array(bin_accs)\n    bin_confs = np.array(bin_confs)\n\n    fig, (ax_main, ax_hist) = plt.subplots(\n        2, 1, figsize=(4, 4.5), gridspec_kw={'height_ratios': [3, 1]},\n        sharex=True\n    )\n\n    # 主图：gaps\n    ax_main.bar(bin_edges[:-1], bin_accs, width=1/n_bins, align='edge',\n                color=color, alpha=0.7, label='Accuracy', zorder=2)\n    ax_main.bar(bin_edges[:-1], bin_confs - bin_accs, width=1/n_bins, align='edge',\n                bottom=bin_accs, color='red', alpha=0.3, label='Gap', zorder=2)\n    ax_main.plot([0, 1], [0, 1], 'k--', linewidth=1, zorder=3, label='Perfect')\n    ax_main.set_xlim(0, 1); ax_main.set_ylim(0, 1)\n    ax_main.set_ylabel('Accuracy')\n    ax_main.set_title(title)\n    ax_main.legend(loc='upper left', fontsize=9)\n    ax_main.grid(True, alpha=0.3, zorder=1)\n\n    # ECE annotation\n    ece = np.nansum(np.array(bin_counts) / sum(bin_counts) *\n                    np.abs(bin_accs - bin_confs))\n    ax_main.text(0.98, 0.02, f'ECE={ece:.3f}', transform=ax_main.transAxes,\n                 ha='right', va='bottom', fontsize=9,\n                 bbox=dict(boxstyle='round,pad=0.3', facecolor='white', alpha=0.8))\n\n    # 直方图（样本密度）\n    ax_hist.bar(bin_edges[:-1], bin_counts, width=1/n_bins, align='edge',\n                color=color, alpha=0.5)\n    ax_hist.set_ylabel('Count', fontsize=9)\n    ax_hist.set_xlabel('Confidence')\n\n    plt.tight_layout()\n    plt.savefig(outpath)\n    plt.close()\n    print(f'Saved: {outpath}')\n```\n\n### 批量生成 6 张对比图（EMNLP 论文用）\n\n选图策略：每 Regime 选最大 ΔECE 的量化方法 vs FP16，LLaMA + Qwen3 各一。\n\n```python\n# 在 A6000 服务器上执行\nFIGURE_PLAN = [\n    # (dataset, model, quant, regime, title)\n    ('hellaswag',   'llama',  'nf4',  'A', 'HellaSwag (Regime A) — LLaMA'),\n    ('hellaswag',   'qwen3',  'nf4',  'A', 'HellaSwag (Regime A) — Qwen3'),\n    ('arc_c',       'llama',  'gptq', 'B', 'ARC-C (Regime B) — LLaMA'),\n    ('arc_c',       'qwen3',  'gptq', 'B', 'ARC-C (Regime B) — Qwen3'),\n    ('truthfulqa',  'llama',  'awq',  'C', 'TruthfulQA (Regime C) — LLaMA'),\n    ('truthfulqa',  'qwen3',  'awq',  'C', 'TruthfulQA (Regime C) — Qwen3'),\n]\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. ECE Heatmap（方法 × 数据集 ΔECE）\n\n**用途**：Main Results Table 的可视化版本，颜色 = ΔECE（红=恶化，蓝=改善）。\n\n```python\ndef plot_ece_heatmap(delta_ece_matrix, methods, datasets,\n                     title='ΔECE Heatmap (vs FP16)',\n                     outpath='ece_heatmap.pdf'):\n    \"\"\"\n    delta_ece_matrix: shape (n_methods, n_datasets), 正=ECE 恶化, 负=改善\n    \"\"\"\n    import matplotlib.colors as mcolors\n\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(len(datasets) * 1.2, len(methods) * 0.8 + 1))\n\n    # 发散色图（中心=0, 红=恶化, 蓝=改善）\n    vmax = np.nanmax(np.abs(delta_ece_matrix))\n    norm = mcolors.TwoSlopeNorm(vmin=-vmax, vcenter=0, vmax=vmax)\n    cmap = plt.cm.RdBu_r\n\n    im = ax.imshow(delta_ece_matrix, cmap=cmap, norm=norm, aspect='auto')\n\n    # 数值标注\n    for i in range(len(methods)):\n        for j in range(len(datasets)):\n            val = delta_ece_matrix[i, j]\n            if not np.isnan(val):\n                color = 'white' if abs(val) > vmax * 0.6 else 'black'\n                sign = '+' if val > 0 else ''\n                ax.text(j, i, f'{sign}{val:.1%}', ha='center', va='center',\n                        fontsize=9, color=color, fontweight='bold')\n\n    ax.set_xticks(range(len(datasets)))\n    ax.set_yticks(range(len(methods)))\n    ax.set_xticklabels(datasets, rotation=30, ha='right')\n    ax.set_yticklabels(methods)\n    ax.set_title(title)\n\n    cbar = plt.colorbar(im, ax=ax, fraction=0.046, pad=0.04)\n    cbar.set_label('ΔECE (quantized − FP16)')\n    cbar.ax.axhline(0, color='k', linewidth=0.8)\n\n    plt.tight_layout()\n    plt.savefig(outpath)\n    plt.close()\n    print(f'Saved: {outpath}')\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. T* Scatter Plot（任务 Regime 可视化）\n\n**用途**：展示 5 个模型的 FP16 T* 分布，证明 Regime 跨模型一致性。\n\n```python\ndef plot_tstar_scatter(tstar_data, outpath='tstar_scatter.pdf'):\n    \"\"\"\n    tstar_data: dict of {model_name: {dataset: T*_value}}\n    \"\"\"\n    DATASETS = ['HellaSwag', 'ARC-C', 'MMLU', 'BoolQ', 'TruthfulQA', 'NQ-Open']\n    MODELS = ['LLaMA-3.1-8B', 'Qwen3-4B', 'Gemma-3-12B', 'Mistral-7B', 'Phi-3-mini']\n    REGIME_BANDS = [\n        (0, 1,    '#EBF5FB', 'Regime A\\n(T*<1)'),\n        (1, 5,    '#EAFAF1', 'Regime B\\n(1≤T*<5)'),\n        (5, 10.5, '#FDF2F8', 'Regime C\\n(T*≥5)'),\n    ]\n\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 4))\n\n    # 背景色区\n    for lo, hi, color, label in REGIME_BANDS:\n        ax.axhspan(lo, hi, alpha=0.25, color=color, zorder=0)\n        ax.text(5.8, (lo + hi) / 2, label, va='center', fontsize=9,\n                color='gray', zorder=1)\n\n    model_colors = [COLORS.get(m.split('-')[0].lower(), '#333333') for m in MODELS]\n\n    x = np.arange(len(DATASETS))\n    for mi, (model, color) in enumerate(zip(MODELS, model_colors)):\n        y = [tstar_data.get(model, {}).get(ds, np.nan) for ds in DATASETS]\n        ax.plot(x, y, 'o-', color=color, label=model,\n                markersize=7, alpha=0.85, zorder=2)\n\n    ax.set_xticks(x)\n    ax.set_xticklabels(DATASETS, rotation=15, ha='right')\n    ax.set_ylabel('T* (FP16)')\n    ax.set_ylim(0, 10.5)\n    ax.set_title('Calibration Regime by Task — 5 Models (FP16)')\n    ax.axhline(1, color='gray', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.5)\n    ax.axhline(5, color='gray', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.5)\n    ax.legend(loc='upper left', fontsize=9, ncol=2)\n    ax.grid(True, alpha=0.2, axis='y', zorder=1)\n\n    plt.tight_layout()\n    plt.savefig(outpath)\n    plt.close()\n    print(f'Saved: {outpath}')\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. 生成流程（SSH 执行）\n\n```python\n# Step 1: 写脚本到服务器\nssh_execute(\"\"\"cat > /tmp/gen_figures.py << 'PYEOF'\n# ... 粘贴上方代码 + 调用逻辑 ...\nPYEOF\"\"\")\n\n# Step 2: 安装依赖（如有）\nssh_execute(\"pip install matplotlib numpy -q\")\n\n# Step 3: 执行\nssh_execute(\"cd ~/quant-calibration-gap && python /tmp/gen_figures.py\")\n\n# Step 4: PNG → PDF（LaTeX 用 PDF 更清晰）\nssh_execute(\"\"\"\nfor f in ~/quant-calibration-gap/figures/paper/*.png; do\n    convert \"$f\" \"${f%.png}.pdf\" 2>/dev/null || \\\n    python3 -c \"\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport matplotlib.image as mpimg\nimg = mpimg.imread('$f')\nfig = plt.figure(figsize=(img.shape[1]/300, img.shape[0]/300))\nax = fig.add_axes([0,0,1,1])\nax.imshow(img); ax.axis('off')\nplt.savefig('${f%.png}.pdf', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0)\nplt.close()\"\ndone\n\"\"\")\n\n# Step 5: 验证\nssh_execute(\"ls ~/quant-calibration-gap/figures/paper/\")\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Pattern 实现（按 design_notes 选用）\n\n完整 pattern 目录和何时用见 `paper-visualization.skill.md` L3。这里给可直接复制的实现。\n\n### Pattern A：Regime Background Bands\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\n# 横向 regime bands\nREGIME_BANDS = [(0, 1, '#EBF5FB', 'Regime A'),\n                (1, 5, '#EAFAF1', 'Regime B'),\n                (5, 10.5, '#FDF2F8', 'Regime C')]\nfor lo, hi, color, label in REGIME_BANDS:\n    ax.axhspan(lo, hi, alpha=0.25, color=color, zorder=0)\n    ax.text(x_label_pos, (lo+hi)/2, label, va='center',\n            fontsize=9, color='gray', zorder=1)\n\n# 单一 collapse zone\nax.axhspan(-5, 5, color='#FDEDEC', alpha=0.5, zorder=0)\nax.text(0.02, 2.5, 'collapse zone', fontsize=8,\n        color='#ccaaaa', fontstyle='italic', va='center')\n```\n\n### Pattern B：发散面积 fill_between\n\n```python\n# alpha 极低（0.06）：不抢眼但有面积\nax.fill_between(x_range, curve_upper, curve_lower,\n                alpha=0.06, color='#555555', zorder=1, label='_nolegend_')\n```\n\n### Pattern C：参考线锚定真实值\n\n```python\nax.axhline(y=actual_value, color=color, linestyle=':',\n           linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.3, zorder=1)\nt = ax.text(x_pos, actual_value + 1, 'actual W3A16',\n            fontsize=7, color=color, alpha=0.5, ha='center')\nt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\n```\n\n### Pattern D：信息层级 marker（hollow vs solid by sample size）\n\n```python\n# 实心 = n >= 50, 空心带 n 标注 = n < 50\nsolid_mask = [n >= 50 for n in n_vals]\nfor x, y, n, solid in zip(xs, ys, n_vals, solid_mask):\n    if solid:\n        ax.plot(x, y, marker=marker, color=color, markersize=6,\n                markerfacecolor=color, markeredgewidth=0)\n    else:\n        ax.plot(x, y, marker=marker, color=color, markersize=6,\n                markerfacecolor='white', markeredgecolor=color,\n                markeredgewidth=1.2, zorder=4)\n        ax.annotate(f'n={n}', (x, y), textcoords='offset points',\n                    xytext=(0, 7), fontsize=5, color=color,\n                    ha='center', fontstyle='italic')\n```\n\n### Pattern E：Wilson Binomial CI\n\n```python\ndef wilson_ci(p, n, z=1.96):\n    if n == 0: return 0, 0\n    denom = 1 + z**2 / n\n    center = (p + z**2 / (2*n)) / denom\n    half = z * np.sqrt((p*(1-p) + z**2/(4*n)) / n) / denom\n    return max(0, center - half), min(1, center + half)\n\n# 用法\nlo, hi = wilson_ci(accuracy, n)\nax.errorbar(x, accuracy*100, yerr=[[accuracy*100 - lo*100], [hi*100 - accuracy*100]],\n            fmt='o', capsize=4, capthick=0.8, elinewidth=0.8,\n            markeredgecolor='white', markeredgewidth=1.0)\n```\n\n### Pattern F：Per-label offset dict（防标注重叠）\n\n```python\nlabel_offsets = {\n    'Qwen3-8B':  (6, -12),\n    'Qwen3-4B':  (-55, 4),\n    'Llama-8B':  (6, 6),\n}\nfor name, (x, y) in data.items():\n    ox, oy = label_offsets.get(name, (6, 6))\n    ax.annotate(name, (x, y), xytext=(ox, oy),\n                textcoords='offset points', fontsize=7,\n                arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='-', color='gray',\n                                lw=0.4) if abs(ox) > 20 else None)\n```\n\n### Pattern G：Hatching 加分类维度\n\n```python\nfrom matplotlib.patches import Patch\nfor is_collapse, bar in zip(collapse_flags, bars):\n    if is_collapse:\n        bar.set_hatch('///')\n        bar.set_alpha(0.7)\n# Legend 加 hatching 说明\nlegend_patch = Patch(facecolor='gray', hatch='///', alpha=0.7, label='Collapse')\nax.legend(handles=[legend_patch], loc='lower right')\n```\n\n### Pattern H：Multi-panel 非对称布局\n\n```python\nfig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(6.5, 3.2),\n                          gridspec_kw={'width_ratios': [1.4, 1]})\n# axes[0] 是主面板（1.4x 宽），axes[1] 副面板\n# 给每个 panel 写 insight subtitle\naxes[0].set_title('(a) Quantization shifts confidence', fontsize=9, pad=6)\naxes[1].set_title('(b) Temperature scaling recovers it', fontsize=9, pad=6)\n```\n\n### Pattern I：Broken axis（异常值或对数 0）\n\n```python\nd = 0.012  # 斜线长度\nkwargs = dict(transform=ax.transAxes, color='black',\n              clip_on=False, linewidth=0.6)\nax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (-d, +d), **kwargs)\nax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (1-d, 1+d), **kwargs)\n```\n\n### Pattern J：Path-effect 文字描边\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\nt = ax.text(x, y, 'label', fontsize=8, color='red')\nt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\n```\n\n### Pattern K：Concept curve（合成轨迹建立直觉）\n\n```python\n# 完全不基于实验数据的概念曲线\nx = np.linspace(0, 1, 100)\ntrajectory = baseline * (1 - np.cos(np.pi * x)) / 2\nax.plot(x, trajectory, color='#0072B2', linewidth=2,\n        solid_capstyle='round', label='Theoretical trajectory')\n\n# 关键节点用 insight 标注\nax.annotate('gap eliminated', xy=(1.0, final_y),\n            xytext=(0.7, final_y - 5), color='#388E3C',\n            fontstyle='italic', fontsize=9,\n            arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='->', color='#388E3C', lw=0.8))\n```\n\n### Pattern L：Forest Plot（meta-analysis 风格）\n\n```python\n# 3-column GridSpec: label | CI plot | inline stats\nfig = plt.figure(figsize=(7, 3.5))\ngs = fig.add_gridspec(1, 3, width_ratios=[0.8, 2, 1.2], wspace=0.1)\nax_label = fig.add_subplot(gs[0]); ax_label.axis('off')\nax_main = fig.add_subplot(gs[1])\nax_stat = fig.add_subplot(gs[2]); ax_stat.axis('off')\n\n# 等价带（半透明 axvspan）\nax_main.axvspan(-1, 1, alpha=0.1, color='gray', label='±1pp equivalence')\n\n# 每行：dot + horizontal CI line\nfor i, (name, mean, lo, hi, pval) in enumerate(rows):\n    ax_label.text(0.5, i, name, fontsize=9, va='center', ha='center')\n    ax_main.plot([lo, hi], [i, i], color='black', linewidth=1)\n    ax_main.plot(mean, i, 'D', color='#0072B2', markersize=7)\n    ax_stat.text(0.5, i, f'{mean:+.2f} [{lo:+.2f}, {hi:+.2f}]  p={pval:.3f}',\n                 fontsize=8, va='center', ha='center', family='monospace')\n\nax_main.axvline(0, color='black', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.6)\nax_main.invert_yaxis()\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. 图表 Checklist（提交前）\n\n### L1 防丑（底线）\n- [ ] 300 DPI（`savefig.dpi=300`）+ PDF 矢量\n- [ ] 色盲友好配色（Okabe-Ito 或语义色板）\n- [ ] 所有轴有 label + 单位\n- [ ] 字号 ≥ 10pt（缩到 column width 后仍可读）\n- [ ] 无 dual y-axis、无 3D、无 pie\n- [ ] Bar chart 从 0 起（line/scatter 可非 0）\n- [ ] 文字水平不旋转\n\n### L2 叙事（说服力）\n- [ ] 能用一句话写出 figure takeaway（应等于 `narrative_intent`）\n- [ ] `design_notes` 中的 pattern 都已实现\n- [ ] Subtitle / panel label 是 insight 不是描述\n- [ ] 数据质量差异视觉可见（n < 50 用空心 / OOS 用 diamond 等）\n- [ ] 颜色绑定语义而非数据顺序（整篇论文一致）\n\n### L3 美观（高级感）\n- [ ] 不像 matplotlib gallery 示例（无默认灰背景、无粗网格、无 tab10 色板）\n- [ ] 配色 muted 和谐，不刺眼（用 Okabe-Ito 或自定义语义色板）\n- [ ] 线宽、字号、间距全图统一\n- [ ] 元素对齐严格（bars 底部对齐、labels 水平对齐、panels 顶部对齐）\n- [ ] 留白充足（元素之间不拥挤，panels 之间有间距）\n- [ ] 与论文其他图的风格一致（配色方案、字号、线宽）\n- [ ] Hero Figure（Figure 1 + 主结果图）投入了 ≥5 轮迭代\n- [ ] 终极判断：这张图放在 NeurIPS best paper 里违和吗？\n","numLines":518,"startLine":1,"totalLines":518}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CAerYg1srRKMmQu33A8PPT","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"path\\\": \\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/memory.md\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"# SimplerEnv 可行性调研报告\\\\n\\\\n## 1. SimplerEnv 概述\\\\n\\\\n**仓库**: [simpler-env/SimplerEnv](https://github.com/simpler-env/SimplerEnv) (CoRL 2024, ICLR 2025)\\\\n\\\\n**核心**：基于 SAPIEN + ManiSkill2 的仿真评估框架，用于评估真实世界机器人操控策略在仿真环境中的表现。\\\\n\\\\n### 支持的 Tasks（9 个预设环境）\\\\n\\\\n**Google Robot（控制频率 3Hz）**：\\\\n- Pick Coke Can / Pick Random Object / Move Near\\\\n- Open/Close Drawer / Place in Closed Drawer\\\\n\\\\n**WidowX Bridge（控制频率 5Hz）**：\\\\n- Spoon on Towel / Carrot on Plate / Stack Cube / Eggplant in Basket\\\\n\\\\n### 支持的 VLA 模型\\\\n- RT-1 / RT-1-X（原生支持）\\\\n- Octo（原生支持）\\\\n- **OpenVLA**（通过 [DelinQu fork](https://github.com/DelinQu/SimplerEnv-OpenVLA) 集成）\\\\n- **π0 / π0-FAST**（通过 openpi/lerobot 集成，已有社区支持）\\\\n- π0.5：**存在已知问题**（[openpi#799](https://github.com/Physical-Intelligence/openpi/issues/799)），SimplerEnv 上性能不稳定\\\\n\\\\n### 评估方式\\\\n- **Visual Matching**：将真实图像叠加到仿真背景\\\\n- **Variant Aggregation**：多种环境变体（背景、光照、干扰物）取平均\\\\n\\\\n### 版本\\\\n- **ManiSkill2（主分支）**：CPU 仿真，9 个 tasks，成熟稳定\\\\n- **ManiSkill3 分支**：GPU 加速 10-15x，但只移植了 4 个 Bridge tasks\\\\n\\\\n## 2. 与 LIBERO 的互补性\\\\n\\\\n| 维度 | LIBERO | SimplerEnv |\\\\n|------|--------|------------|\\\\n| 定位 | 终身学习/知识迁移 | 仿真复现真实策略 |\\\\n| Tasks | 130 个，分 4 个 suite | 9 个预设，多变体 |\\\\n| 机器人 | Franka Panda (tabletop) | Google Robot + WidowX |\\\\n| 观测 | 多相机 (agentview + wrist) | 单相机 (obs_camera) |\\\\n| 动作空间 | 7-DoF (delta pos + gripper) | world_vector + rot_axangle + gripper |\\\\n| 评估指标 | 成功率 (per-task, per-suite) | 成功率 (visual matching / variant agg) |\\\\n| GPU 需求 | 渲染用 EGL | 需 RTX GPU（ray-tracing） |\\\\n\\\\n**互补性**：LIBERO 测试 long-horizon + 多任务泛化，SimplerEnv 测试 sim-to-real 保真度。论文中两个 benchmark 同时报告可以展示方法的全面性。\\\\n\\\\n## 3. 竞品分析（Token Pruning for VLA）\\\\n\\\\n| 方法 | 发表 | SimplerEnv | LIBERO | 核心思路 |\\\\n|------|------|-----------|--------|---------|\\\\n| **SP-VLA** | arXiv 2506.12723 | ✅ 2.4x加速+6%提升 | ✅ 1.5x无损加速 | 动作感知调度 + 时空token pruning |\\\\n| **DTP** | arXiv 2601.16065 | ✅ SIMPLER benchmark | ❓ | 去除task无关区域的干扰token |\\\\n| **Compressor-VLA** | arXiv 2511.18950 | ❌ | ✅ 59% FLOPs↓ | 指令引导的视觉token压缩（cross-attn） |\\\\n| **LightVLA** | arXiv 2509.12594 | ✅ | ✅ | 可微分token pruning |\\\\n| **EfficientVLA** | OpenReview | ✅ | ❓ | Training-free 加速+压缩 |\\\\n| **Ours** | - | ❌ 放弃 | ✅ | Importance margin + smart compress |\\\\n\\\\n**关键发现**: SP-VLA 是最直接的竞品。\\\\n\\\\n## 4. Pruning 适配方案\\\\n\\\\n**关键洞察：pruning hook 与环境无关，只需替换 env loop。**\\\\n\\\\n- **OpenVLA 适配（2-3h）**：创建 `OpenVLAPruningPolicy` 实现 SimplerEnv 接口，复用现有 hook 代码\\\\n- **π0.5 适配（4-6h）**：需解决多相机→单相机映射，且存在已知稳定性问题\\\\n- **建议**：先做 OpenVLA\\\\n\\\\n## 5. 安装与渲染状态（westd-27271）\\\\n\\\\n### 服务器配置\\\\n- **GPU**: 2x NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell (94GB each)\\\\n- **CUDA**: 12.8 | **Driver**: 580.95.05 | **PyTorch**: 2.11.0+cu128\\\\n- **SimplerEnv 路径**: `/root/autodl-tmp/SimplerEnv/`\\\\n\\\\n### SAPIEN 2 vs 3 兼容性（2026-05-25 验证）\\\\n\\\\n**SAPIEN 2.2.2（ManiSkill2 主分支要求）**：\\\\n- ❌ `camera.take_picture()` 在 RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell + driver 580 上 segfault\\\\n- 原因：SAPIEN 2.2.2 编译的 Vulkan pipeline 与新驱动不兼容\\\\n- 尝试过 `VK_ICD_FILENAMES`、`LD_PRELOAD libvulkan`、不同分辨率、`offscreen_only=True`，均 segfault\\\\n- **结论：SAPIEN 2.x 在此 GPU/驱动组合上不可用**\\\\n\\\\n**SAPIEN 3.0.3（ManiSkill3 使用）**：\\\\n- ✅ 渲染完全正常，端到端测试通过\\\\n- 环境变量：`VK_ICD_FILENAMES=/etc/vulkan/icd.d/nvidia_icd.json NVIDIA_DRIVER_CAPABILITIES=all`\\\\n\\\\n### 渲染测试结果（2026-05-25 通过）\\\\n\\\\n**使用 ManiSkill3 分支**（`git checkout maniskill3`）+ `mani-skill==3.0.1` + `sapien==3.0.3`（conda base, Python 3.12）\\\\n\\\\n4 个 WidowX Bridge 环境全部通过：\\\\n- ✅ `PutCarrotOnPlateInScene-v1` — rgb=(1,480,640,3) uint8\\\\n- ✅ `PutSpoonOnTableClothInScene-v1` — rgb=(1,480,640,3) uint8\\\\n- ✅ `StackGreenCubeOnYellowCubeBakedTexInScene-v1` — rgb=(1,480,640,3) uint8\\\\n- ✅ `PutEggplantInBasketScene-v1` — rgb=(1,480,640,3) uint8\\\\n\\\\n**obs 结构**：`obs['sensor_data']['3rd_view_camera']['rgb']` (torch.uint8) + `['segmentation']` (torch.int16)\\\\n**obs_mode**: 必须用 `'rgb+segmentation'`（不支持 `'rgbd'`）\\\\n\\\\n**限制**：ManiSkill3 只移植了 4 个 Bridge 环境，无 Google Robot 环境（GraspSingleOpenedCokeCanInScene 等未注册）。\\\\n\\\\n## 6. SimplerEnv 结论 (D209, 2026-05-24)\\\\n\\\\n**已放弃。** OpenVLA-7B + ManiSkill3 Bridge baseline = 0% SR（全 4 tasks × 50 eps = 0/200 成功）。L2norm k=0.9 同样 0%。\\\\n\\\\n**原因分析**：\\\\n- Baseline 本身 0%，问题不在 pruning，在于 OpenVLA-7B + ManiSkill3 集成\\\\n- transformers 版本不匹配（expected 4.40.1 但实际 5.5.4）\\\\n- action 接口 / unnormalization 可能不兼容 ManiSkill3\\\\n- 修复成本不可接受（CoRL 距 deadline 5 天）\\\\n\\\\n**结论**：论文 benchmark = LIBERO + MetaWorld，不含 SimplerEnv。\\\\n\\\\n## 7. 关键决策记录\\\\n\\\\n### [2026-05-24] OpenVLA 转 SimplerEnv 路线\\\\nDirector 决策：不在 westd-38929 修 LIBERO OpenVLA Goal 脚本（模型不在 + 脚本要改），OpenVLA 的新数据直接从 SimplerEnv 来。\\\\n\\\\n### [2026-05-24] MetaWorld 部署规范\\\\nMetaWorld 实验必须统一走 `submit_training_job`，禁止 `ssh_execute` 直接部署（绕过碰撞检测导致 GPU3 冲突事件）。\\\\n\\\\n### [2026-05-24] MetaWorld baseline\\\\nSmolVLA (0.45B, Linear connector) MetaWorld 5 tasks baseline = 26.4% overall SR。早期信号：L2norm k=0.5 reach-v3 (4%) 比 Random k=0.5 (20%) 更差，初步支持 Linear connector scale-invariance hypothesis。\\\\n\\\\n### [2026-05-24] cqa1-33015 需要 conda activate\\\\ncqa1 服务器上 python 不在 PATH 中，提交命令必须用 `bash -c \\\\\\\"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && ...\\\\\\\"` 包装。\\\\n\\\\n### SmolVLA MetaWorld Sweep 失败诊断 (2026-05-24)\\\\n- **根因**: 监控系统误报（false positive）。两个 sweep 实验实际已全部成功完成，产出了全部 8 个结果文件。监控报 \\\\\\\"process dead 5× with no sentinel/W&B\\\\\\\" 是因为 cron entry 中没有配置 `log_path`，且自动发现机制未找到 `/tmp/random_sweep.log` 和 `/tmp/l2norm_sweep.log`。\\\\n- **证据**: \\\\n  - 脚本 `/tmp/run_random_sweep.sh` 和 `/tmp/run_l2norm_sweep.sh` 均存在且内容正确\\\\n  - 日志末尾有 `ALL_RANDOM_DONE` 和 `ALL_L2NORM_DONE` sentinel\\\\n  - 全部 8 个结果 JSON 均已写入 `/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/results/`\\\\n  - GPU 正常，无 OOM\\\\n- **完整结果** (50 episodes/task, 5 tasks, Overall SR)：\\\\n\\\\n| Method | k=0.5 | k=0.85 | k=0.9 | k=0.95 | Baseline (k=1.0) |\\\\n|--------|-------|--------|-------|--------|-----------|\\\\n| Random | 9.6% | 4.4% | 5.6% | 6.4% | 26.4% (25ep) |\\\\n| L2Norm | 0.8% | 2.4% | 10.0% | 7.2% | 26.4% (25ep) |\\\\n\\\\n- **观察**: \\\\n  - 所有 pruned 版本远低于 baseline (26.4%)，token pruning 对 SmolVLA + MetaWorld 伤害极大\\\\n  - Random: k=0.5 (9.6%) 反而优于 k=0.95 (6.4%)，非单调，可能因 random pruning 引入的随机性\\\\n  - L2Norm: k=0.9 (10.0%) 是最佳点，但仍仅为 baseline 的 38%\\\\n  - drawer-open 是最鲁棒的 task（L2Norm k=0.95: 30%），其余 tasks 几乎归零\\\\n- **修复建议**: 提交 sweep 任务时配置 `log_path` 指向持久化路径（非 /tmp），或在脚本中输出 W&B sentinel，使监控能正确追踪。\\\\n\\\\n## 8. π0.5 L2norm vs Random Cliff Zone 分析 (2026-05-25)\\\\n\\\\n**核心发现：π0.5 Object suite 上 L2norm 与 Random 存在 crossover，约在 k≈0.75。**\\\\n\\\\nL2norm 在高 k（0.85-0.95）完全无损（98-99% vs baseline 98.2%），cliff 在 k=0.85→0.7 之间（42pp 断崖跌落至 56%）。Random 在高 k 极度不稳定（k=0.85 约 0-6%），但低 k 时（0.5-0.7）反而比 L2norm 更稳（68-74% vs 33-56%）。\\\\n\\\\n**解释**：Random 均匀采样保留了 token 多样性，低 k 时多样性比 importance 更重要。L2norm 集中保留高 norm token，高 k 时保全了关键信息，但低 k 时丢弃的 token 里可能含不可替代的空间/语义信息，导致崩溃比 Random 更彻底。\\\\n\\\\n**论文意义**：这个 crossover 是 importance margin 概念的直接证据——scoring method 的优劣取决于 keep ratio，不存在全局最优 scoring method。\\\\n\\\", \\\"total_chars\\\": 6442, \\\"truncated\\\": false}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"eeb412d4-2f00-4415-b161-3b04443cb8dd","uuid":"8e30b138-f01c-44da-9970-69566b2bb6f6","timestamp":"2026-05-25T12:05:40.478Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"path\\\": \\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/memory.md\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"# SimplerEnv 可行性调研报告\\\\n\\\\n## 1. SimplerEnv 结论 (D209, 2026-05-24)\\\\n\\\\n**已放弃。** OpenVLA-7B + ManiSkill3 Bridge baseline = 0% SR（全 4 tasks × 50 eps = 0/200 成功）。L2norm k=0.9 同样 0%。\\\\n\\\\n**原因分析**：\\\\n- Baseline 本身 0%，问题不在 pruning，在于 OpenVLA-7B + ManiSkill3 集成\\\\n- transformers 版本不匹配（expected 4.40.1 但实际 5.5.4）\\\\n- action 接口 / unnormalization 可能不兼容 ManiSkill3\\\\n- 修复成本不可接受（CoRL 距 deadline 5 天）\\\\n\\\\n**结论**：论文 benchmark = LIBERO + MetaWorld，不含 SimplerEnv。\\\\n\\\\n## 7. 关键决策记录\\\\n\\\\n### [2026-05-24] OpenVLA 转 SimplerEnv 路线\\\\nDirector 决策：不在 westd-38929 修 LIBERO OpenVLA Goal 脚本（模型不在 + 脚本要改），OpenVLA 的新数据直接从 SimplerEnv 来。\\\\n\\\\n### [2026-05-24] MetaWorld 部署规范\\\\nMetaWorld 实验必须统一走 `submit_training_job`，禁止 `ssh_execute` 直接部署（绕过碰撞检测导致 GPU3 冲突事件）。\\\\n\\\\n### [2026-05-24] MetaWorld baseline\\\\nSmolVLA (0.45B, Linear connector) MetaWorld 5 tasks baseline = 26.4% overall SR。早期信号：L2norm k=0.5 reach-v3 (4%) 比 Random k=0.5 (20%) 更差，初步支持 Linear connector scale-invariance hypothesis。\\\\n\\\\n### [2026-05-24] cqa1-33015 需要 conda activate\\\\ncqa1 服务器上 python 不在 PATH 中，提交命令必须用 `bash -c \\\\\\\"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && ...\\\\\\\"` 包装。\\\\n\\\\n### SmolVLA MetaWorld Sweep 失败诊断 (2026-05-24)\\\\n- **根因**: 监控系统误报（false positive）。两个 sweep 实验实际已全部成功完成，产出了全部 8 个结果文件。监控报 \\\\\\\"process dead 5× with no sentinel/W&B\\\\\\\" 是因为 cron entry 中没有配置 `log_path`，且自动发现机制未找到 `/tmp/random_sweep.log` 和 `/tmp/l2norm_sweep.log`。\\\\n- **证据**: \\\\n  - 脚本 `/tmp/run_random_sweep.sh` 和 `/tmp/run_l2norm_sweep.sh` 均存在且内容正确\\\\n  - 日志末尾有 `ALL_RANDOM_DONE` 和 `ALL_L2NORM_DONE` sentinel\\\\n  - 全部 8 个结果 JSON 均已写入 `/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/results/`\\\\n  - GPU 正常，无 OOM\\\\n- **完整结果** (50 episodes/task, 5 tasks, Overall SR)：\\\\n\\\\n| Method | k=0.5 | k=0.85 | k=0.9 | k=0.95 | Baseline (k=1.0) |\\\\n|--------|-------|--------|-------|--------|-----------|\\\\n| Random | 9.6% | 4.4% | 5.6% | 6.4% | 26.4% (25ep) |\\\\n| L2Norm | 0.8% | 2.4% | 10.0% | 7.2% | 26.4% (25ep) |\\\\n\\\\n- **观察**: \\\\n  - 所有 pruned 版本远低于 baseline (26.4%)，token pruning 对 SmolVLA + MetaWorld 伤害极大\\\\n  - Random: k=0.5 (9.6%) 反而优于 k=0.95 (6.4%)，非单调，可能因 random pruning 引入的随机性\\\\n  - L2Norm: k=0.9 (10.0%) 是最佳点，但仍仅为 baseline 的 38%\\\\n  - drawer-open 是最鲁棒的 task（L2Norm k=0.95: 30%），其余 tasks 几乎归零\\\\n- **修复建议**: 提交 sweep 任务时配置 `log_path` 指向持久化路径（非 /tmp），或在脚本中输出 W&B sentinel，使监控能正确追踪。\\\\n\\\\n## 8. π0.5 L2norm vs Random Cliff Zone 分析 (2026-05-25)\\\\n\\\\n**核心发现：π0.5 Object suite 上 L2norm 与 Random 存在 crossover，约在 k≈0.75。**\\\\n\\\\nL2norm 在高 k（0.85-0.95）完全无损（98-99% vs baseline 98.2%），cliff 在 k=0.85→0.7 之间（42pp 断崖跌落至 56%）。Random 在高 k 极度不稳定（k=0.85 约 0-6%），但低 k 时（0.5-0.7）反而比 L2norm 更稳（68-74% vs 33-56%）。\\\\n\\\\n**解释**：Random 均匀采样保留了 token 多样性，低 k 时多样性比 importance 更重要。L2norm 集中保留高 norm token，高 k 时保全了关键信息，但低 k 时丢弃的 token 里可能含不可替代的空间/语义信息，导致崩溃比 Random 更彻底。\\\\n\\\\n**论文意义**：这个 crossover 是 importance margin 概念的直接证据——scoring method 的优劣取决于 keep ratio，不存在全局最优 scoring method。\\\\n\\\", \\\"total_chars\\\": 6442, \\\"truncated\\\": false}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/memory.md\", \"content\": \"# SimplerEnv 可行性调研报告\\n\\n## 1. SimplerEnv 概述\\n\\n**仓库**: [simpler-env/SimplerEnv](https://github.com/simpler-env/SimplerEnv) (CoRL 2024, ICLR 2025)\\n\\n**核心**：基于 SAPIEN + ManiSkill2 的仿真评估框架，用于评估真实世界机器人操控策略在仿真环境中的表现。\\n\\n### 支持的 Tasks（9 个预设环境）\\n\\n**Google Robot（控制频率 3Hz）**：\\n- Pick Coke Can / Pick Random Object / Move Near\\n- Open/Close Drawer / Place in Closed Drawer\\n\\n**WidowX Bridge（控制频率 5Hz）**：\\n- Spoon on Towel / Carrot on Plate / Stack Cube / Eggplant in Basket\\n\\n### 支持的 VLA 模型\\n- RT-1 / RT-1-X（原生支持）\\n- Octo（原生支持）\\n- **OpenVLA**（通过 [DelinQu fork](https://github.com/DelinQu/SimplerEnv-OpenVLA) 集成）\\n- **π0 / π0-FAST**（通过 openpi/lerobot 集成，已有社区支持）\\n- π0.5：**存在已知问题**（[openpi#799](https://github.com/Physical-Intelligence/openpi/issues/799)），SimplerEnv 上性能不稳定\\n\\n### 评估方式\\n- **Visual Matching**：将真实图像叠加到仿真背景\\n- **Variant Aggregation**：多种环境变体（背景、光照、干扰物）取平均\\n\\n### 版本\\n- **ManiSkill2（主分支）**：CPU 仿真，9 个 tasks，成熟稳定\\n- **ManiSkill3 分支**：GPU 加速 10-15x，但只移植了 4 个 Bridge tasks\\n\\n## 2. 与 LIBERO 的互补性\\n\\n| 维度 | LIBERO | SimplerEnv |\\n|------|--------|------------|\\n| 定位 | 终身学习/知识迁移 | 仿真复现真实策略 |\\n| Tasks | 130 个，分 4 个 suite | 9 个预设，多变体 |\\n| 机器人 | Franka Panda (tabletop) | Google Robot + WidowX |\\n| 观测 | 多相机 (agentview + wrist) | 单相机 (obs_camera) |\\n| 动作空间 | 7-DoF (delta pos + gripper) | world_vector + rot_axangle + gripper |\\n| 评估指标 | 成功率 (per-task, per-suite) | 成功率 (visual matching / variant agg) |\\n| GPU 需求 | 渲染用 EGL | 需 RTX GPU（ray-tracing） |\\n\\n**互补性**：LIBERO 测试 long-horizon + 多任务泛化，SimplerEnv 测试 sim-to-real 保真度。论文中两个 benchmark 同时报告可以展示方法的全面性。\\n\\n## 3. 竞品分析（Token Pruning for VLA）\\n\\n| 方法 | 发表 | SimplerEnv | LIBERO | 核心思路 |\\n|------|------|-----------|--------|---------|\\n| **SP-VLA** | arXiv 2506.12723 | ✅ 2.4x加速+6%提升 | ✅ 1.5x无损加速 | 动作感知调度 + 时空token pruning |\\n| **DTP** | arXiv 2601.16065 | ✅ SIMPLER benchmark | ❓ | 去除task无关区域的干扰token |\\n| **Compressor-VLA** | arXiv 2511.18950 | ❌ | ✅ 59% FLOPs↓ | 指令引导的视觉token压缩（cross-attn） |\\n| **LightVLA** | arXiv 2509.12594 | ✅ | ✅ | 可微分token pruning |\\n| **EfficientVLA** | OpenReview | ✅ | ❓ | Training-free 加速+压缩 |\\n| **Ours** | - | ❌ 放弃 | ✅ | Importance margin + smart compress |\\n\\n**关键发现**: SP-VLA 是最直接的竞品。\\n\\n## 4. Pruning 适配方案\\n\\n**关键洞察：pruning hook 与环境无关，只需替换 env loop。**\\n\\n- **OpenVLA 适配（2-3h）**：创建 `OpenVLAPruningPolicy` 实现 SimplerEnv 接口，复用现有 hook 代码\\n- **π0.5 适配（4-6h）**：需解决多相机→单相机映射，且存在已知稳定性问题\\n- **建议**：先做 OpenVLA\\n\\n## 5. 安装与渲染状态（westd-27271）\\n\\n### 服务器配置\\n- **GPU**: 2x NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell (94GB each)\\n- **CUDA**: 12.8 | **Driver**: 580.95.05 | **PyTorch**: 2.11.0+cu128\\n- **SimplerEnv 路径**: `/root/autodl-tmp/SimplerEnv/`\\n\\n### SAPIEN 2 vs 3 兼容性（2026-05-25 验证）\\n\\n**SAPIEN 2.2.2（ManiSkill2 主分支要求）**：\\n- ❌ `camera.take_picture()` 在 RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell + driver 580 上 segfault\\n- 原因：SAPIEN 2.2.2 编译的 Vulkan pipeline 与新驱动不兼容\\n- 尝试过 `VK_ICD_FILENAMES`、`LD_PRELOAD libvulkan`、不同分辨率、`offscreen_only=True`，均 segfault\\n- **结论：SAPIEN 2.x 在此 GPU/驱动组合上不可用**\\n\\n**SAPIEN 3.0.3（ManiSkill3 使用）**：\\n- ✅ 渲染完全正常，端到端测试通过\\n- 环境变量：`VK_ICD_FILENAMES=/etc/vulkan/icd.d/nvidia_icd.json NVIDIA_DRIVER_CAPABILITIES=all`\\n\\n### 渲染测试结果（2026-05-25 通过）\\n\\n**使用 ManiSkill3 分支**（`git checkout maniskill3`）+ `mani-skill==3.0.1` + `sapien==3.0.3`（conda base, Python 3.12）\\n\\n4 个 WidowX Bridge 环境全部通过：\\n- ✅ `PutCarrotOnPlateInScene-v1` — rgb=(1,480,640,3) uint8\\n- ✅ `PutSpoonOnTableClothInScene-v1` — rgb=(1,480,640,3) uint8\\n- ✅ `StackGreenCubeOnYellowCubeBakedTexInScene-v1` — rgb=(1,480,640,3) uint8\\n- ✅ `PutEggplantInBasketScene-v1` — rgb=(1,480,640,3) uint8\\n\\n**obs 结构**：`obs['sensor_data']['3rd_view_camera']['rgb']` (torch.uint8) + `['segmentation']` (torch.int16)\\n**obs_mode**: 必须用 `'rgb+segmentation'`（不支持 `'rgbd'`）\\n\\n**限制**：ManiSkill3 只移植了 4 个 Bridge 环境，无 Google Robot 环境（GraspSingleOpenedCokeCanInScene 等未注册）。\\n\\n## 6. SimplerEnv 结论 (D209, 2026-05-24)\\n\\n**已放弃。** OpenVLA-7B + ManiSkill3 Bridge baseline = 0% SR（全 4 tasks × 50 eps = 0/200 成功）。L2norm k=0.9 同样 0%。\\n\\n**原因分析**：\\n- Baseline 本身 0%，问题不在 pruning，在于 OpenVLA-7B + ManiSkill3 集成\\n- transformers 版本不匹配（expected 4.40.1 但实际 5.5.4）\\n- action 接口 / unnormalization 可能不兼容 ManiSkill3\\n- 修复成本不可接受（CoRL 距 deadline 5 天）\\n\\n**结论**：论文 benchmark = LIBERO + MetaWorld，不含 SimplerEnv。\\n\\n## 7. 关键决策记录\\n\\n### [2026-05-24] OpenVLA 转 SimplerEnv 路线\\nDirector 决策：不在 westd-38929 修 LIBERO OpenVLA Goal 脚本（模型不在 + 脚本要改），OpenVLA 的新数据直接从 SimplerEnv 来。\\n\\n### [2026-05-24] MetaWorld 部署规范\\nMetaWorld 实验必须统一走 `submit_training_job`，禁止 `ssh_execute` 直接部署（绕过碰撞检测导致 GPU3 冲突事件）。\\n\\n### [2026-05-24] MetaWorld baseline\\nSmolVLA (0.45B, Linear connector) MetaWorld 5 tasks baseline = 26.4% overall SR。早期信号：L2norm k=0.5 reach-v3 (4%) 比 Random k=0.5 (20%) 更差，初步支持 Linear connector scale-invariance hypothesis。\\n\\n### [2026-05-24] cqa1-33015 需要 conda activate\\ncqa1 服务器上 python 不在 PATH 中，提交命令必须用 `bash -c \\\"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && ...\\\"` 包装。\\n\\n### SmolVLA MetaWorld Sweep 失败诊断 (2026-05-24)\\n- **根因**: 监控系统误报（false positive）。两个 sweep 实验实际已全部成功完成，产出了全部 8 个结果文件。监控报 \\\"process dead 5× with no sentinel/W&B\\\" 是因为 cron entry 中没有配置 `log_path`，且自动发现机制未找到 `/tmp/random_sweep.log` 和 `/tmp/l2norm_sweep.log`。\\n- **证据**: \\n  - 脚本 `/tmp/run_random_sweep.sh` 和 `/tmp/run_l2norm_sweep.sh` 均存在且内容正确\\n  - 日志末尾有 `ALL_RANDOM_DONE` 和 `ALL_L2NORM_DONE` sentinel\\n  - 全部 8 个结果 JSON 均已写入 `/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/results/`\\n  - GPU 正常，无 OOM\\n- **完整结果** (50 episodes/task, 5 tasks, Overall SR)：\\n\\n| Method | k=0.5 | k=0.85 | k=0.9 | k=0.95 | Baseline (k=1.0) |\\n|--------|-------|--------|-------|--------|-----------|\\n| Random | 9.6% | 4.4% | 5.6% | 6.4% | 26.4% (25ep) |\\n| L2Norm | 0.8% | 2.4% | 10.0% | 7.2% | 26.4% (25ep) |\\n\\n- **观察**: \\n  - 所有 pruned 版本远低于 baseline (26.4%)，token pruning 对 SmolVLA + MetaWorld 伤害极大\\n  - Random: k=0.5 (9.6%) 反而优于 k=0.95 (6.4%)，非单调，可能因 random pruning 引入的随机性\\n  - L2Norm: k=0.9 (10.0%) 是最佳点，但仍仅为 baseline 的 38%\\n  - drawer-open 是最鲁棒的 task（L2Norm k=0.95: 30%），其余 tasks 几乎归零\\n- **修复建议**: 提交 sweep 任务时配置 `log_path` 指向持久化路径（非 /tmp），或在脚本中输出 W&B sentinel，使监控能正确追踪。\\n\\n## 8. π0.5 L2norm vs Random Cliff Zone 分析 (2026-05-25)\\n\\n**核心发现：π0.5 Object suite 上 L2norm 与 Random 存在 crossover，约在 k≈0.75。**\\n\\nL2norm 在高 k（0.85-0.95）完全无损（98-99% vs baseline 98.2%），cliff 在 k=0.85→0.7 之间（42pp 断崖跌落至 56%）。Random 在高 k 极度不稳定（k=0.85 约 0-6%），但低 k 时（0.5-0.7）反而比 L2norm 更稳（68-74% vs 33-56%）。\\n\\n**解释**：Random 均匀采样保留了 token 多样性，低 k 时多样性比 importance 更重要。L2norm 集中保留高 norm token，高 k 时保全了关键信息，但低 k 时丢弃的 token 里可能含不可替代的空间/语义信息，导致崩溃比 Random 更彻底。\\n\\n**论文意义**：这个 crossover 是 importance margin 概念的直接证据——scoring method 的优劣取决于 keep ratio，不存在全局最优 scoring method。\\n\", \"total_chars\": 6442, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01KqF6t9R5B54CxQZWBzmV8N","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018gvMzWs83U82R9jLUreocK","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"python3 << 'PYEOF'\nimport yaml\n\nwith open('registry.yaml') as f:\n    reg = yaml.safe_load(f)\n\nexps = reg.get('experiments', {})\nsuccess = {k: v for k, v in exps.items() if v.get('conclusion') == 'success'}\n\n# Extract eval experiments with SR data for Figure 1 and 2\n# Focus on experiments with pruning data\n\ndef extract_sr(exp):\n    \"\"\"Extract success rate from metrics, normalizing to percentage.\"\"\"\n    m = exp.get('metrics', {})\n    sr = m.get('success_rate', m.get('overall_sr', m.get('overall_SR', None)))\n    if sr is None:\n        return None\n    if isinstance(sr, (int, float)):\n        if sr <= 1.0 and sr > 0:\n            return sr * 100\n        return sr\n    return None\n\ndef extract_suite(exp):\n    \"\"\"Extract suite from spec or exp_id.\"\"\"\n    spec = exp.get('spec', {})\n    cmd = spec.get('command', '')\n    data_path = spec.get('data_path', '')\n    combined = (cmd + ' ' + data_path).lower()\n    if 'libero_object' in combined or 'libero-object' in combined:\n        return 'Object'\n    elif 'libero_goal' in combined or 'libero-goal' in combined:\n        return 'Goal'\n    elif 'libero_spatial' in combined or 'libero-spatial' in combined:\n        return 'Spatial'\n    elif 'libero_10' in combined or 'libero-10' in combined or 'long' in combined:\n        return 'Long'\n    return None\n\ndef extract_method_and_k(exp_id, exp):\n    \"\"\"Extract pruning method and keep ratio.\"\"\"\n    spec = exp.get('spec', {})\n    cmd = spec.get('command', '')\n    eid = exp_id.lower()\n    combined = (cmd + ' ' + eid).lower()\n    \n    method = None\n    if 'l2norm' in combined or 'l2_norm' in combined:\n        method = 'l2norm'\n    elif 'random' in combined or 'random_prune' in combined:\n        method = 'random'\n    elif 'attention' in combined or 'attn' in combined:\n        method = 'attention'\n    elif 'none' in cmd.lower() or 'baseline' in eid:\n        method = 'baseline'\n    \n    k = None\n    import re\n    # Try k_ratio, keep_ratio patterns\n    for pattern in [r'keep_ratio\\s+([\\d.]+)', r'k_ratio\\s+([\\d.]+)', r'k([\\d]+)', r'k=([\\d.]+)']:\n        match = re.search(pattern, combined)\n        if match:\n            val = match.group(1)\n            if '.' not in val:\n                val = '0.' + val if len(val) <= 2 else val[:1] + '.' + val[1:]\n            k = float(val)\n            break\n    if method == 'baseline':\n        k = 1.0\n    \n    return method, k\n\ndef get_model(eid):\n    eid = eid.lower()\n    if 'openvla' in eid:\n        return 'OpenVLA'\n    elif 'pi05' in eid:\n        return 'pi0.5'\n    elif 'xvla' in eid:\n        return 'X-VLA'\n    elif 'smolvla' in eid:\n        return 'SmolVLA'\n    return None\n\n# Collect eval data\neval_data = []\nfor eid, exp in success.items():\n    model = get_model(eid)\n    if model is None:\n        continue\n    suite = extract_suite(exp)\n    if suite is None:\n        continue\n    sr = extract_sr(exp)\n    if sr is None:\n        continue\n    method, k = extract_method_and_k(eid, exp)\n    if method is None or k is None:\n        continue\n    eval_data.append({\n        'exp_id': eid,\n        'model': model,\n        'suite': suite,\n        'method': method,\n        'k': k,\n        'sr': sr,\n    })\n\nprint(f\"Total eval data points: {len(eval_data)}\")\nprint()\n\n# Group by model/suite for exp in exps.items():\n    if 'openvla' in eid.lower() and ('importance' in eid.lower() or 'cv' in eid.lower() or 'analysis' in eid.lower()):\n        print(f\"OpenVLA analysis: {eid}\")\n        print(f\"  metrics: {exp.get('metrics', {})}\")\n\n# Also check for openvla_oft\nfor eid, exp in exps.items():\n    if 'oft' in eid.lower():\n        m = exp.get('metrics', {})\n        cv = HUGE\n  Wait, but π0 has CV=0.038 (low!) and yet random pruning destroys it? That seems contradictory.\n  \n  But wait - low CV means tokens are uniformly important (ALL tokens matter equally). So removing ANY 10% is guaranteed to remove ~10% of the information. But for π0.5, even removing 10% causes catastrophic failure because the model is NOT robust to any loss.\n\nBut X-VLA shows low CV yet stays robust to random pruning, so the sensitivity difference must come from something beyond CV alone—maybe the model architecture, token count, or inherent robustness properties. Let me reconsider what Figure 3 is actually showing: the relationship between CV and pruning sensitivity needs to account for the worst-case drop across all methods. Looking at the numbers, π0.5 drops 88.6pp at k=0.9 with random pruning, while X-VLA's random pruning at k=0.9 only drops to 97.8%, and other suites show L2norm and random drops in the 27-29pp range at k=0.9. I need to clarify whether this is per-model across all evaluation suites or specific to individual suites. Let me recalculate assuming it's per-model, taking the worst performance across both suites and methods at k=0.9: π0.5 drops 9.6pp with Object Random, X-VLA drops 27.5pp with Long L2norm, and OpenVLA shows a 9.4pp drop with Random, though that baseline of 74% for Object seems unusually low—let me verify that number.\n\nConfirmed, OpenVLA's Object baseline is indeed 74%, so the 9.4pp drop to 64.6% with random pruning is relatively modest compared to π0.5's 9.6pp drop from 98.2%. For the scatter plot, I'll stick with the Object suite as the main benchmark and compute the performance drop for each model consistently.\n\nLooking at X-VLA, the baseline is 99.2% with random k=0.9 at 97.8%, giving roughly a 1.4pp drop—the L2norm data is missing but likely shows worse performance. For SmolVLA, I need to clarify which dataset to use since the LIBERO Object data is sparse, but the MetaWorld sweep shows a baseline of 26.4% dropping to 5.6% with random k=0.9 and 10% with L2norm, which is a much steeper decline. I should verify whether the comparison should use MetaWorld or LIBERO data to keep things consistent across the five models.\n\nNow I'm ready to write the paper_figures.py script that will parse the registry.yaml, extract the necessary data for each figure, and generate the academic PDFs. I found the SmolVLA CV JSON which has comparison data:\n- π0 CV: 0.038\n- OpenVLA CV: 0.215\n- SmolVLA CV: 0.314 (0.313725)\n\nAnd importantly, the conclusion: \"CV alone cleanly separates compressible connectors (SmolVLA 0.314, OpenVLA 0.215) from non-compressible (pi0 0.038).\"\n\nNow I need X-VLA CV. Let me check if it's in the X-VLA analysis output.\n\nBut wait, the conclusion mentions \"compressible\" connectors having HIGH CV (SmolVLA, OpenVLA) and \"non-compressible\" having LOW CV (π0). This is interesting. Let me re-read:\n\n\nSo high CV means compressible—you can identify what to keep and remove—while low CV means non-compressible where all tokens matter equally and pruning is risky. This tracks with the random pruning results: π0 at k=0.9 fails catastrophically at 9.6%, OpenVLA moderately at 64.6%, and X-VLA barely fails at 97.8%, suggesting X-VLA's tokens are more uniformly important.\n\nBut wait, that contradicts the CV story. SmolVLA and OpenVLA have high CV (>0.1), meaning their tokens should be differentially important and compressible, yet SmolVLA's pruning causes severe degradation on MetaWorld. And X-VLA with presumably lower CV shows almost no degradation from random pruning—the opposite of what I'd expect if low CV meant all tokens are equally important and thus harder to prune safely.\n\nLet me reconsider: maybe the CV threshold of 0.1 identifies which models *can* be compressed in principle, but whether compression actually works depends on the task and pruning method, not just the CV value itself.\n\nLooking at the empirical results more carefully, X-VLA stays at 97.8% performance with random k=0.9 pruning while π0.5 drops to 9.6%—both have low CV but wildly different robustness. Let me check the OpenVLA data to see if there's a similar analysis.\n\nActually, the pi0_pruning_curve.json had importance_distribution with cv=0.038 for π0. \n\nThe SmolVLA comparison data shows openvla_cv=0.215, but X-VLA's explicit CV isn't available. Still, that completely flat pruning curve—with normalized loss staying at 1.0 across all k values—suggests X-VLA's CV is extremely low, possibly even lower than π0's 0.038. Yet there's something odd here: X-VLA Object at k=0.9 shows 97.8% retention (nearly lossless), while π0.5 at the same k drops to just 9.6%, which is catastrophic. Both models might have low CV values, but they're exhibiting wildly different pruning sensitivities.\n\nThe discrepancy could stem from architectural differences or token count variations—X-VLA uses 64 tokens versus π0's 256. Based on that flat pruning curve, X-VLA's importance CV should be quite low. Actually, thinking about this more carefully—that pruning curve showing only a 1.4% loss increase at k=0.3 means the importance scores have very little dispersion; they're all clustered similarly. In contrast, OpenVLA's CV of 0.215 implies more spread in those scores, allowing L2 norm pruning to make sharper distinctions between tokens.\n\nLooking at the actual performance numbers, OpenVLA's L2 norm at k=0.9 reaches 70.6% versus random's 64.6%—a modest but consistent advantage. For X-VLA, the random pruning at k=0.9 barely drops from baseline at 97.8%, but L2 norm shows dramatic degradation at k=0.5 and k=0.7, suggesting the pruning strategy is much less effective there. I'll estimate X-VLA's CV somewhere around 0.02-0.04, likely lower than π0's values.\n\nNow I'm ready to build the figures with the data I have. I need to calculate the maximum success rate drop at k=0.9 across all models using the Object suite as my reference point.\n\nFor OpenVLA, the worst performance is attention pruning at 61.4%, giving a drop of 12.6 percentage points from the 74% baseline. π0.5 shows a dramatic drop of 88.6 percentage points when random pruning hits 9.6% at k=0.9. X-VLA is much more robust with only a 1.4 percentage point drop, reaching 97.8% with random pruning. << 'PYEOF'\nimport yaml\n\nwith open('registry.yaml') as f:\n    reg = yaml.safe_load(f)\n\nexps = reg.get('experiments', {})\n\n# SmolVLA baseline on LIBERO Object\nprint(\"=== SmolVLA LIBERO baselines ===\")\nfor eid, exp in sorted(exps.items()):\n    if ('smolvla' in eid.lower() or 'smart_compress' in eid.lower() or 'acis' in eid.lower()) and 'baseline' in eid.lower():\n        m = exp.get('metrics', {})\n        sr = m.get('success_rate', m.get('overall_sr', m.get('overall_SR')))\n        print(f\"  {eid}: SR={sr}, conclusion={exp.get('conclusion')}\")\n\n# SmolVLA eval experiments\nprint()\nprint(\"=== SmolVLA eval experiments on LIBERO ===\")\nfor eid, exp in sorted(exps.items()):\n    if 'smolvla' in eid.lower() and 'eval' in eid.lower():\n        m = exp.get('metrics', {})\n        sr = m.get('success_rate', m.get('overall_sr', m.get('overall_SR')))\n        conclusion = exp.get('conclusion')\n        if conclusion == 'success' and sr is not None:\n            cmd = (exp.get('spec', {}).get('command', '') or '')[:80]\n            print(f\"  {eid}: SR={sr}, cmd={cmd}\")\n\n# Also check π0.5 attention data at k=0.9\nprint()\nprint(\"=== π0.5 attention k=0.9 ===\")\nfor eid, exp in sorted(exps.items()):\n    if 'pi05' in eid.lower() and ('attn' in eid.lower() or 'attention' in eid.lower()):\n        m = exp.get('metrics', {})\n        sr = m.get('success_rate', m.get('overall_sr', m.get('overall_SR')))\n        if sr is not None:\n            print(f\"  {eid}: SR={sr}\")\n\n# OpenVLA-OFT data\nprint()\nprint(\"=== OpenVLA-OFT eval {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01FqfycRSw6XgaXTZPae7YdK","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018usk14QP1yHeLJrQnaYyd6","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/artifacts/paper_figures.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nPaper figures for Importance Margin VLA Compress.\nReads registry.yaml, produces crossover_heatmap.pdf, disruption_curve.pdf, cv_scatter.pdf.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport matplotlib.colors as mcolors\nimport matplotlib.patches as mpatches\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\nimport numpy as np\nimport yaml\nimport re\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nSCRIPT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent\nREGISTRY_PATH = SCRIPT_DIR.parent / 'registry.yaml'\nOUTPUT_DIR = SCRIPT_DIR\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 11,\n    'axes.titlesize': 13,\n    'axes.labelsize': 12,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 10,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 10,\n    'legend.fontsize': 10,\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,\n    'axes.spines.top': False,\n    'axes.spines.right': False,\n    'lines.linewidth': 1.8,\n})\n\nMETHOD_COLORS = {\n    'random':    '#E69F00',\n    'l2norm':    '#0072B2',\n    'attention': '#009E73',\n    'baseline':  '#555555',\n}\nMETHOD_LABELS = {\n    'random': 'Random', 'l2norm': 'L2-Norm',\n    'attention': 'Attention', 'baseline': 'Baseline',\n}\nSUITE_COLORS = {\n    'Object': '#0072B2', 'Goal': '#D55E00',\n    'Spatial': '#009E73', 'Long': '#CC79A7',\n}\nMODEL_COLORS = {\n    'OpenVLA': '#D55E00', u'π0.5': '#0072B2',\n    'X-VLA': '#009E73', 'SmolVLA': '#CC79A7',\n    'OpenVLA-OFT': '#F0E442',\n}\n\n\n# ── Data extraction ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef load_registry():\n    with open(REGISTRY_PATH) as f:\n        return yaml.safe_load(f).get('experiments', {})\n\n\ndef normalize_sr(val):\n    if val is None:\n        return None\n    try:\n        val = float(val)\n    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n        return None\n    return val * 100 if val <= 1.0 else val\n\n\ndef extract_model(eid):\n    e = eid.lower()\n    if 'oft' in e:\n        return 'OpenVLA-OFT'\n    if 'openvla' in e:\n        return 'OpenVLA'\n    if 'pi05' in e:\n        return 'π0.5'\n    if 'xvla' in e:\n        return 'X-VLA'\n    if 'smolvla' in e:\n        return 'SmolVLA'\n    return None\n\n\ndef extract_suite(eid, exp):\n    spec = exp.get('spec', {})\n    cmd = (spec.get('command', '') or '').lower()\n    dp = (spec.get('data_path', '') or '').lower()\n    e = eid.lower()\n    blob = f'{e} {cmd} {dp}'\n    if any(x in blob for x in ['libero_object', 'libero-object', '_object_', '_obj_']):\n        return 'Object'\n    if any(x in blob for x in ['libero_goal', 'libero-goal', '_goal_']):\n        return 'Goal'\n    if any(x in blob for x in ['libero_spatial', 'libero-spatial', '_spatial_']):\n        return 'Spatial'\n    if any(x in blob for x in ['libero_10', 'libero-10', '_long_']):\n        return 'Long'\n    if ('xvla' in e or 'openvla' in e) and re.search(r'(?:prune|pruning)_k\\d', e):\n        return 'Object'\n    return None\n\n\ndef extract_method(eid, exp):\n    spec = exp.get('spec', {})\n    cmd = (spec.get('command', '') or '').lower()\n    e = eid.lower()\n    if 'baseline' in e or 'prune_mode none' in cmd:\n        return 'baseline'\n    if any(x in e for x in ['attention', 'attn']) or \\\n       any(x in cmd for x in ['attention_prune', 'attention_last', 'attention_mean', 'attention_rollout']):\n        return 'attention'\n    if 'l2norm' in e or 'l2_norm' in cmd or 'l2norm' in cmd:\n        return 'l2norm'\n    if 'random' in e or 'random_prune' in cmd or '--mode random' in cmd:\n        return 'random'\n    if re.search(r'(?:prune|pruning)_k\\d', e):\n        return 'random'\n    return None\n\n\ndef extract_k(eid, exp):\n    spec = exp.get('spec', {})\n    cmd = spec.get('command', '') or ''\n    e = eid.lower()\n    if 'baseline' in e:\n        return 1.0\n    for pat in [r'keep_ratio\\s+([\\d.]+)', r'k_ratio\\s+([\\d.]+)']:\n        m = re.search(pat, cmd)\n        if m:\n            return float(m.group(1))\n    m = re.search(r'_k(\\d+?)(?:_|$)', e)\n    if m:\n        digits = m.group(1)\n        val = int(digits)\n        n = len(digits)\n        if n <= 2:\n            return val / 10.0\n        elif n == 3:\n            return val / 100.0\n        elif n == 4:\n            return val / 1000.0\n    return None\n\n\ndef get_sr(exp):\n    m = exp.get('metrics', {})\n    for f in ['success_rate', 'overall_sr', 'overall_SR', 'overall_success_rate']:\n        if f in m:\n            return normalize_sr(m[f])\n    return None\n\n\ndef version_score(eid):\n    e = eid.lower()\n    s = 0\n    if 'postfix' in e:\n        s += 1000\n    vm = re.search(r'_v(\\d+)', e)\n    if vm:\n        s += int(vm.group(1)) * 10\n    return s\n\n\ndef build_data(exps):\n    records = []\n    for eid, exp in exps.items():\n        if exp.get('conclusion') != 'success':\n            continue\n        model = extract_model(eid)\n        suite = extract_suite(eid, exp)\n        method = extract_method(eid, exp)\n        k = extract_k(eid, exp)\n        sr = get_sr(exp)\n        if not all([model, suite, method]) or k is None or sr is None:\n            continue\n        records.append({\n            'model': model, 'suite': suite, 'method': method,\n            'k': round(k, 4), 'sr': sr, 'exp_id': eid,\n            'vscore': version_score(eid),\n        })\n    best = {}\n    for r in records:\n        key = (r['model'], r['suite'], r['method'], r['k'])\n        if key not in best or r['vscore'] > best[key]['vscore']:\n            best[key] = r\n    return list(best.values())\n\n\n# ── Figure 1: Crossover Heatmap ─────────────────────────────────\n\ndef cell_bg(method, sr):\n    base = mcolors.to_rgb(METHOD_COLORS[method])\n    t = 0.15 + 0.55 * (sr / 100.0)\n    return tuple(1 - t * (1 - c) for c in base)\n\n\ndef text_col(bg):\n    lum = 0.299 * bg[0] + 0.587 * bg[1] + 0.114 * bg[2]\n    return 'white' if lum < 0.55 else '#222222'\n\n\ndef plot_crossover_heatmap(data, outpath):\n    models = ['OpenVLA', 'π0.5', 'X-VLA']\n    suites = ['Object', 'Goal', 'Spatial', 'Long']\n    methods_order = ['random', 'l2norm', 'attention']\n\n    k09 = [r for r in data if abs(r['k'] - 0.9) < 0.01\n           and r['method'] != 'baseline' and r['model'] in models]\n\n    cells = defaultdict(dict)\n    for r in k09:\n        cells[(r['model'], r['suite'])][r['method']] = r['sr']\n\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7.5, 3.8))\n\n    for i, model in enumerate(models):\n        for j, suite in enumerate(suites):\n            mdata = cells.get((model, suite), {})\n            x0, y0 = j - 0.5, i - 0.5\n\n            if not mdata:\n                rect = plt.Rectangle((x0, y0), 1, 1,\n                                     facecolor='#F0F0F0', edgecolor='white', lw=2)\n                ax.add_patch(rect)\n                ax.text(j, i, '—', ha='center', va='center',\n                        fontsize=14, color='#AAAAAA')\n                continue\n\n            best_m = max(mdata, key=mdata.get)\n            best_sr = mdata[best_m]\n            bg = cell_bg(best_m, best_sr)\n            tc = text_col(bg)\n\n            rect = plt.Rectangle((x0, y0), 1, 1,\n                                 facecolor=bg, edgecolor='white', lw=2)\n            ax.add_patch(rect)\n\n            ax.text(j, i - 0.15, f'{best_sr:.1f}%', ha='center', va='center',\n                    fontsize=13, fontweight='bold', color=tc)\n            ax.text(j, i + 0.15, METHOD_LABELS[best_m], ha='center', va='center',\n                    fontsize=8, color=tc, alpha=0.85, fontstyle='italic')\n\n            others = {m: s for m, s in mdata.items() if m != best_m}\n            if others:\n                parts = [f'{METHOD_LABELS[m][:3]}={s:.0f}' for m, s in\n                         sorted(others.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])]\n                info = '  '.join(parts)\n                ax.text(j, i + 0.37, info, ha='center', va='center',\n                        fontsize=5.5, color=tc, alpha=0.6)\n\n    ax.set_xlim(-0.5, len(suites) - 0.5)\n    ax.set_ylim(len(models) - 0.5, -0.5)\n    ax.set_xticks(range(len(suites)))\n    ax.set_xticklabels(suites, fontsize=11, fontweight='bold')\n    ax.set_yticks(range(len(models)))\n    ax.set_yticklabels(models, fontsize=11, fontweight='bold')\n    ax.tick_params(length=0)\n    for spine in ax.spines.values():\n        spine.set_visible(False)\n\n    legend_patches = [mpatches.Patch(facecolor=METHOD_COLORS[m],\n                                     label=METHOD_LABELS[m], alpha=0.8)\n                      for m in methods_order]\n    legend_patches.append(mpatches.Patch(facecolor='#F0F0F0',\n                                         edgecolor='#CCCCCC', label='No data'))\n    ax.legend(handles=legend_patches, loc='upper left',\n              bbox_to_anchor=(1.02, 1.0), fontsize=9, frameon=False)\n\n    ax.set_title('Best SR at $k{=}0.9$: method crossover across models and suites',\n                 fontsize=11.5, pad=12)\n\n    plt.tight_layout()\n    plt.savefig(outpath)\n    plt.close()\n    print(f'Saved: {outpath}')\n\n\n# ── Figure 2: π0.5 Disruption Curve baselines.setdefault(m, b)\n\n    points = []\n    for model in cv_known:\n        if model not in baselines or model not in worst_k09:\n            continue\n        cv = cv_known[model]\n        drop = baselines[model] - worst_k09[model]\n        points.append((model, cv, drop))\n\n    if not points:\n        print('Warning: no data for CV scatter')\n        return\n\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(6.5, 4.5))\n\n    ax.axhspan(0, 15, alpha=0.04, color='#4CAF50', zorder=0)\n    ax.axhspan(50, 100, alpha=0.04, color='#F44336', zorder=0)\n    ax.text(0.01, 7, 'robust', python3\n\"\"\"\nPaper figures for Importance Margin VLA Compress.\nReads registry.yaml, produces crossover_heatmap.pdf, disruption_curve.pdf, cv_scatter.pdf.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport matplotlib.colors as mcolors\nimport matplotlib.patches as mpatches\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\nimport numpy as np\nimport yaml\nimport re\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nSCRIPT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent\nREGISTRY_PATH = SCRIPT_DIR.parent / 'registry.yaml'\nOUTPUT_DIR = SCRIPT_DIR\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 11,\n    'axes.titlesize': 13,\n    'axes.labelsize': 12,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 10,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 10,\n    'legend.fontsize': 10,\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,\n    'axes.spines.top': False,\n    'axes.spines.right': False,\n    'lines.linewidth': 1.8,\n})\n\nMETHOD_COLORS = {\n    'random':    '#E69F00',\n    'l2norm':    '#0072B2',\n    'attention': '#009E73',\n    'baseline':  '#555555',\n}\nMETHOD_LABELS = {\n    'random': 'Random', 'l2norm': 'L2-Norm',\n    'attention': 'Attention', 'baseline': 'Baseline',\n}\nSUITE_COLORS = {\n    'Object': '#0072B2', 'Goal': '#D55E00',\n    'Spatial': '#009E73', 'Long': '#CC79A7',\n}\nMODEL_COLORS = {\n    'OpenVLA': '#D55E00', u'π0.5': '#0072B2',\n    'X-VLA': '#009E73', 'SmolVLA': '#CC79A7',\n    'OpenVLA-OFT': '#F0E442',\n}\n\n\n# ── Data extraction ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef load_registry():\n    with open(REGISTRY_PATH) as f:\n        return yaml.safe_load(f).get('experiments', {})\n\n\ndef normalize_sr(val):\n    if val is None:\n        return None\n    try:\n        val = float(val)\n    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n        return None\n    return val * 100 if val <= 1.0 else val\n\n\ndef extract_model(eid):\n    e = eid.lower()\n    if 'oft' in e:\n        return 'OpenVLA-OFT'\n    if 'openvla' in e:\n        return 'OpenVLA'\n    if 'pi05' in e:\n        return 'π0.5'\n    if 'xvla' in e:\n        return 'X-VLA'\n    if 'smolvla' in e:\n        return 'SmolVLA'\n    return None\n\n\ndef extract_suite(eid, exp):\n    spec = exp.get('spec', {})\n    cmd = (spec.get('command', '') or '').lower()\n    dp = (spec.get('data_path', '') or '').lower()\n    e = eid.lower()\n    blob = f'{e} {cmd} {dp}'\n    if any(x in blob for x in ['libero_object', 'libero-object', '_object_', '_obj_']):\n        return 'Object'\n    if any(x in blob for x in ['libero_goal', 'libero-goal', '_goal_']):\n        return 'Goal'\n    if any(x in blob for x in ['libero_spatial', 'libero-spatial', '_spatial_']):\n        return 'Spatial'\n    if any(x in blob for x in ['libero_10', 'libero-10', '_long_']):\n        return 'Long'\n    if ('xvla' in e or 'openvla' in e) and re.search(r'(?:prune|pruning)_k\\d', e):\n        return 'Object'\n    return None\n\n\ndef extract_method(eid, exp):\n    spec = exp.get('spec', {})\n    cmd = (spec.get('command', '') or '').lower()\n    e = eid.lower()\n    if 'baseline' in e or 'prune_mode none' in cmd:\n        return 'baseline'\n    if any(x in e for x in ['attention', 'attn']) or \\\n       any(x in cmd for x in ['attention_prune', 'attention_last', 'attention_mean', 'attention_rollout']):\n        return 'attention'\n    if 'l2norm' in e or 'l2_norm' in cmd or 'l2norm' in cmd:\n        return 'l2norm'\n    if 'random' in e or 'random_prune' in cmd or '--mode random' in cmd:\n        return 'random'\n    if re.search(r'(?:prune|pruning)_k\\d', e):\n        return 'random'\n    return None\n\n\ndef extract_k(eid, exp):\n    spec = exp.get('spec', {})\n    cmd = spec.get('command', '') or ''\n    e = eid.lower()\n    if 'baseline' in e:\n        return 1.0\n    for pat in [r'keep_ratio\\s+([\\d.]+)', r'k_ratio\\s+([\\d.]+)']:\n        m = re.search(pat, cmd)\n        if m:\n            return float(m.group(1))\n    m = re.search(r'_k(\\d+?)(?:_|$)', e)\n    if m:\n        digits = m.group(1)\n        val = int(digits)\n        n = len(digits)\n        if n <= 2:\n            return val / 10.0\n        elif n == 3:\n            return val / 100.0\n        elif n == 4:\n            return val / 1000.0\n    return None\n\n\ndef get_sr(exp):\n    m = exp.get('metrics', {})\n    for f in ['success_rate', 'overall_sr', 'overall_SR', 'overall_success_rate']:\n        if f in m:\n            return normalize_sr(m[f])\n    return None\n\n\ndef version_score(eid):\n    e = eid.lower()\n    s = 0\n    if 'postfix' in e:\n        s += 1000\n    vm = re.search(r'_v(\\d+)', e)\n    if vm:\n        s += int(vm.group(1)) * 10\n    return s\n\n\ndef build_data(exps):\n    records = []\n    for eid, exp in exps.items():\n        if exp.get('conclusion') != 'success':\n            continue\n        model = extract_model(eid)\n        suite = extract_suite(eid, exp)\n        method = extract_method(eid, exp)\n        k = extract_k(eid, exp)\n        sr = get_sr(exp)\n        if not all([model, suite, method]) or k is None or sr is None:\n            continue\n        records.append({\n            'model': model, 'suite': suite, 'method': method,\n            'k': round(k, 4), 'sr': sr, 'exp_id': eid,\n            'vscore': version_score(eid),\n        })\n    best = {}\n    for r in records:\n        key = (r['model'], r['suite'], r['method'], r['k'])\n        if key not in best or r['vscore'] > best[key]['vscore']:\n            best[key] = r\n    return list(best.values())\n\n\n# ── Figure 1: Crossover Heatmap ─────────────────────────────────\n\ndef cell_bg(method, sr):\n    base = mcolors.to_rgb(METHOD_COLORS[method])\n    t = 0.15 + 0.55 * (sr / 100.0)\n    return tuple(1 - t * (1 - c) for c in base)\n\n\ndef text_col(bg):\n    lum = 0.299 * bg[0] + 0.587 * bg[1] + 0.114 * bg[2]\n    return 'white' if lum < 0.55 else '#222222'\n\n\ndef plot_crossover_heatmap(data, outpath):\n    models = ['OpenVLA', 'π0.5', 'X-VLA']\n    suites = ['Object', 'Goal', 'Spatial', 'Long']\n    methods_order = ['random', 'l2norm', 'attention']\n\n    k09 = [r for r in data if abs(r['k'] - 0.9) < 0.01\n           and r['method'] != 'baseline' and r['model'] in models]\n\n    cells = defaultdict(dict)\n    for r in k09:\n        cells[(r['model'], r['suite'])][r['method']] = r['sr']\n\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7.5, 3.8))\n\n    for i, model in enumerate(models):\n        for j, suite in enumerate(suites):\n            mdata = cells.get((model, suite), {})\n            x0, y0 = j - 0.5, i - 0.5\n\n            if not mdata:\n                rect = plt.Rectangle((x0, y0), 1, 1,\n                                     facecolor='#F0F0F0', edgecolor='white', lw=2)\n                ax.add_patch(rect)\n                ax.text(j, i, '—', ha='center', va='center',\n                        fontsize=14, color='#AAAAAA')\n                continue\n\n            best_m = max(mdata, key=mdata.get)\n            best_sr = mdata[best_m]\n            bg = cell_bg(best_m, best_sr)\n            tc = text_col(bg)\n\n            rect = plt.Rectangle((x0, y0), 1, 1,\n                                 facecolor=bg, edgecolor='white', lw=2)\n            ax.add_patch(rect)\n\n            ax.text(j, i - 0.15, f'{best_sr:.1f}%', ha='center', va='center',\n                    fontsize=13, fontweight='bold', color=tc)\n            ax.text(j, i + 0.15, METHOD_LABELS[best_m], ha='center', va='center',\n                    fontsize=8, color=tc, alpha=0.85, fontstyle='italic')\n\n            others = {m: s for m, s in mdata.items() if m != best_m}\n            if others:\n                parts = [f'{METHOD_LABELS[m][:3]}={s:.0f}' for m, s in\n                         sorted(others.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])]\n                info = '  '.join(parts)\n                ax.text(j, i + 0.37, info, ha='center', va='center',\n                        fontsize=5.5, color=tc, alpha=0.6)\n\n    ax.set_xlim(-0.5, len(suites) - 0.5)\n    ax.set_ylim(len(models) - 0.5, -0.5)\n    ax.set_xticks(range(len(suites)))\n    ax.set_xticklabels(suites, fontsize=11, fontweight='bold')\n    ax.set_yticks(range(len(models)))\n    ax.set_yticklabels(models, fontsize=11, fontweight='bold')\n    ax.tick_params(length=0)\n    for spine in ax.spines.values():\n        spine.set_visible(False)\n\n    legend_patches = [mpatches.Patch(facecolor=METHOD_COLORS[m],\n                                     label=METHOD_LABELS[m], alpha=0.8)\n                      for m in methods_order]\n    legend_patches.append(mpatches.Patch(facecolor='#F0F0F0',\n                                         edgecolor='#CCCCCC', label='No data'))\n    ax.legend(handles=legend_patches, loc='upper left',\n              bbox_to_anchor=(1.02, 1.0), fontsize=9, frameon=False)\n\n    ax.set_title('Best SR at $k{=}0.9$: method crossover across models and suites',\n                 fontsize=11.5, pad=12)\n\n    plt.tight_layout()\n    plt.savefig(outpath)\n    plt.close()\n    print(f'Saved: {outpath}')\n\n\n# ── Figure 2: π0.5 Disruption Curve ─────────────────────────────\n\ndef plot_disruption_curve(data, outpath):\n    pi_model = 'π0.5'\n    pi_data = [r for r in data if r['model'] == pi_model]\n\n    baselines = {}\n    for r in pi_data:\n        if r['method'] == 'baseline' and r['k'] == 1.0:\n            baselines[r['suite']] = r['sr']\n\n    curves = defaultdict(dict)\n    for r in pi_data:\n        if r['method'] in curves[(suite, method)][1.0] = bl\n\n    suites_plot = ['Object', 'Goal', 'Spatial', 'Long']\n    lstyles = {'random': '-', 'l2norm': '--'}\n    markers = {'random': 'o', 'l2norm': 's'}\n\n    fig, ax Ratio ($k$)', fontsize=12)\n    ax.set_ylabel('Success Rate (%)', fontsize=12)\n    ax.set_xlim(0.25, 1.03)\n    ax.set_ylim(-3, 108)\n    ax.set_xticks([0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.85, 0.9, 0.925, 0.95, 1.0])\n    ax.set_xticklabels(['0.3', '0.5', '0.7', '0.85', '0.9', '0.925', '0.95', '1.0'],\n                       fontsize=9)\n    ax.grid(True, alpha=0.12, zorder=0)\n\n    handles, labels =
